effect_calc_wood_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = wood_delta
		remove_variable = wood_balance_right
		remove_variable = wood_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:wood = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { wood_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = wood_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = wood_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = wood_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:wood_delta
							add = var:wood_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:wood = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:wood_balance_mid } }
					limit = { wood_production_delta > PREV.var:wood_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = wood_delta value = var:wood_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = wood_balance_right value = var:wood_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_camp is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = wood_delta add = PREV.queued_wood } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_greenhouse is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = wood_delta add = PREV.queued_wood_greenhouse } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { OR = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } var:const_to_seed > 0 } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = wood_delta subtract = PREV.queued_construction_wood } }
			}
			# here wood_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = wood_delta	value = 0 } }
	}
}
effect_build_wood = {	# country_scope
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = effect_build_wood.tt
		effect_calc_wood_delta = yes
		while = {
			limit = { var:wood_delta < 0 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_state = {
						OR = {
							any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_camp			can_queue_building_levels = 1 }
							AND = {	# Some states are too small to make use of the resource(s) their state region provides
								has_potential_resource = bg_logging
								NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_camp	OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level = 0 }	can_queue_building_levels = 1 }
							AND = {	# Some states are too small to make use of the resource(s) their state region provides
								has_potential_resource = bg_logging
								NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
							}
						}
					}
					start_building_construction = building_logging_camp
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = wood_delta add = wood_per_level } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						OWNER.var:wood_delta < 0
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_greenhouse OR = { occupancy > 0.95 level = 0 } }
					}
					start_building_construction = building_logging_greenhouse
					OWNER = { change_variable = { name = wood_delta add = wood_per_greenhouse_level } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

}

effect_calc_fertilizer_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = fertilizer_delta
		remove_variable = fertilizer_balance_right
		remove_variable = fertilizer_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:fertilizer = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { fertilizer_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = fertilizer_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = fertilizer_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = fertilizer_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:fertilizer_delta
							add = var:fertilizer_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:fertilizer = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:fertilizer_balance_mid } }
					limit = { fertilizer_production_delta > PREV.var:fertilizer_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = fertilizer_delta value = var:fertilizer_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = fertilizer_balance_right value = var:fertilizer_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_chemical_plants is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = fertilizer_delta add = PREV.queued_fertilizer } }
			}
			# here fertilizer_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = fertilizer_delta	value = 0 } }
	}
}

effect_calc_explosives_delta = {	# Thx to Pzixel
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_variable = explosives_delta
		remove_variable = explosives_balance_right
		remove_variable = explosives_balance_mid
		if = {
			# limit = { market.mg:explosives = { market_goods_delta < 0 } }
			limit = { explosives_production_delta < 0 }
			set_variable = { name = explosives_delta			value =-1000000 }
			set_variable = { name = explosives_balance_right	value =       0 }
			while = {		# use binary search to find the amount at which market_goods_delta is 0
				count = 21	# log2(1000000) + 1
				set_variable = {
					name = explosives_balance_mid
					value = {
						add = {
							value = var:explosives_delta
							add = var:explosives_balance_right
							divide = 2
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					# limit = { market.mg:explosives = { market_goods_delta > PREV.var:explosives_balance_mid } }
					limit = { explosives_production_delta > PREV.var:explosives_balance_mid }
					set_variable = { name = explosives_delta value = var:explosives_balance_mid }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { name = explosives_balance_right value = var:explosives_balance_mid } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_explosives_factory is_under_construction = yes } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = explosives_delta add = PREV.queued_explosives } }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { OR = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector is_under_construction = yes } var:const_to_seed > 0 } }
				OWNER = { change_variable = { name = explosives_delta subtract = PREV.queued_construction_explosives } }
			}
			# here explosives_delta holds the exact value of market_goods_delta (up to integer precision)
		}
		else = { set_variable = { name = explosives_delta	value = 0 } }
	}
}

effect_calc_construction_goods = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_construction_sector } }
		set_variable = {
			name = wood_target
			value = {
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_construction_sector = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					value = b:building_construction_sector.level_after_queued_constructions
					subtract = b:building_construction_sector.level
				}
				else = { value = b:building_construction_sector.level }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = const_to_seed } min = var:const_to_seed }
				multiply = wood_per_const
				divide = wood_per_level	# has null-check built-in
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					subtract = {
						value		= b:building_logging_camp.level_after_queued_constructions
						subtract	= b:building_logging_camp.level
					}
				}
				ceiling = yes
				min = 0
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = fabric_target
			value = {
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_construction_sector = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					value = b:building_construction_sector.level_after_queued_constructions
					subtract = b:building_construction_sector.level
				}
				else = { value = b:building_construction_sector.level }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = const_to_seed } min = var:const_to_seed }
				multiply = fabric_per_const
				divide = fabric_per_level	# has null-check built-in
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_cotton_greenhouse = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					subtract = {
						value		= b:building_cotton_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
						subtract	= b:building_cotton_greenhouse.level
					}
				}
				ceiling = yes
				min = 0
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = tools_target
			value = {
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_construction_sector = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					value = b:building_construction_sector.level_after_queued_constructions
					subtract = b:building_construction_sector.level
				}
				else = { value = b:building_construction_sector.level }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = const_to_seed } min = var:const_to_seed }
				multiply = tools_per_const
				divide = tools_per_level	# has null-check built-in
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_tooling_workshops = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					subtract = {
						value		= b:building_tooling_workshops.level_after_queued_constructions
						subtract	= b:building_tooling_workshops.level
					}
				}
				ceiling = yes
				min = 0
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = glass_target
			value = {
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_construction_sector = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					value = b:building_construction_sector.level_after_queued_constructions
					subtract = b:building_construction_sector.level
				}
				else = { value = b:building_construction_sector.level }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = const_to_seed } min = var:const_to_seed }
				multiply = glass_per_const
				divide = glass_per_level	# has null-check built-in
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_glassworks = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					subtract = {
						value		= b:building_glassworks.level_after_queued_constructions
						subtract	= b:building_glassworks.level
					}
				}
				ceiling = yes
				min = 0
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = steel_target
			value = {
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_construction_sector = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					value = b:building_construction_sector.level_after_queued_constructions
					subtract = b:building_construction_sector.level
				}
				else = { value = b:building_construction_sector.level }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = const_to_seed } min = var:const_to_seed }
				multiply = steel_per_const
				divide = steel_per_level	# has null-check built-in
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_steel_mills = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					subtract = {
						value		= b:building_steel_mills.level_after_queued_constructions
						subtract	= b:building_steel_mills.level
					}
				}
				ceiling = yes
				min = 0
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = explosives_target
			value = {
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_construction_sector = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					value = b:building_construction_sector.level_after_queued_constructions
					subtract = b:building_construction_sector.level
				}
				else = { value = b:building_construction_sector.level }
				if = { limit = { has_variable = const_to_seed } min = var:const_to_seed }
				multiply = explosives_per_const
				divide = explosives_per_level	# has null-check built-in
				if = {
					limit = { b:building_explosives_factory = { is_under_construction = yes } }
					subtract = {
						value		= b:building_explosives_factory.level_after_queued_constructions
						subtract	= b:building_explosives_factory.level
					}
				}
				ceiling = yes
				min = 0
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_build_construction_goods_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:wood_target > 0
			OR = {
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_camp			can_queue_building_levels = 1 }
				AND = {
					has_potential_resource = bg_logging
					NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
				}
			}
		}
		start_building_construction = building_logging_camp
		change_variable = { name = wood_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:fabric_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_cotton_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = fabric_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:tools_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_tooling_workshops
		change_variable = { name = tools_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:glass_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_glassworks
		change_variable = { name = glass_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:steel_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_steel_mills
		change_variable = { name = steel_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:explosives_target > 0 }
		start_building_construction = building_explosives_factory
		change_variable = { name = explosives_target subtract = 1 }
	}
}
effect_build_construction_goods_private_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:wood_target > 0
			OR = {
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_logging_camp			can_queue_building_levels = 1 }
				AND = {
					has_potential_resource = bg_logging
					NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
				}
			}
		}
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_logging_camp
		change_variable = { name = wood_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:fabric_target > 0 }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_cotton_greenhouse
		change_variable = { name = fabric_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:tools_target > 0 }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_tooling_workshops
		change_variable = { name = tools_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:glass_target > 0 }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_glassworks
		change_variable = { name = glass_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:steel_target > 0 }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_steel_mills
		change_variable = { name = steel_target subtract = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:explosives_target > 0 }
		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_explosives_factory
		change_variable = { name = explosives_target subtract = 1 }
	}
}
effect_fix_construction_goods_tooltip = {
	# custom_label = "If a state cannot sustain enough $building_logging_camp$, greenhouses will fill the deficit instead."
	show_as_tooltip = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes trigger_microstate = no }
			if = {
				limit = { var:wood_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:wood_target			start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_logging_camp }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:fabric_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:fabric_target			start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_cotton_greenhouse }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:tools_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:tools_target			start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_tooling_workshops }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:glass_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:glass_target			start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_glassworks }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:steel_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:steel_target			start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_steel_mills }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:explosives_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:explosives_target		start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_explosives_factory }
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no trigger_microstate = no }
			if = {
				limit = { var:wood_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:wood_target			start_building_construction = building_logging_camp }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:fabric_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:fabric_target			start_building_construction = building_cotton_greenhouse }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:tools_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:tools_target			start_building_construction = building_tooling_workshops }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:glass_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:glass_target			start_building_construction = building_glassworks }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:steel_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:steel_target			start_building_construction = building_steel_mills }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { var:explosives_target > 0 }
				while = { count = var:explosives_target		start_building_construction = building_explosives_factory }
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_fix_construction_goods_shortage = {	# TODO: Rework to use country_scope variables that are incrementally decreased when queueing construction
	effect_calc_construction_goods			= yes
	effect_fix_construction_goods_tooltip	= yes
	hidden_effect = {
		while = {
			limit = { any_scope_state = { OR = { var:wood_target > 0 var:fabric_target > 0 var:tools_target > 0 var:glass_target > 0 var:steel_target > 0 var:explosives_target > 0 } } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes trigger_microstate = no }
				effect_build_construction_goods_private_state = yes
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no trigger_microstate = no }
				effect_build_construction_goods_state = yes
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { var:wood_target > 0 }
			set_variable = {
				name = wood_greenhouse_target
				value = {
					value = wood_target
					multiply = wood_per_level
					divide = wood_per_greenhouse_level	# has null-check built-in
					if = {
						limit = { b:building_logging_greenhouse = { is_under_construction = yes } }
						subtract = {
							value		= b:building_logging_greenhouse.level_after_queued_constructions
							subtract	= b:building_logging_greenhouse.level
						}
					}
					min = 0
				}
			}
			set_variable = { name = wood_target value = 0 }
		}
		while = {
			limit = { any_scope_state = { var:wood_greenhouse_target > 0 } }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { var:wood_greenhouse_target > 0 trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = yes trigger_microstate = no }
				start_privately_funded_building_construction = building_logging_greenhouse
				change_variable = { name = wood_greenhouse_target subtract = 1 }
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { var:wood_greenhouse_target > 0 trigger_incorporated_or_incorporating = no trigger_microstate = no }
				start_building_construction = building_logging_greenhouse
				change_variable = { name = wood_greenhouse_target subtract = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_loans_setup = {
	if	= { limit	= { NOT = { has_variable = loans_var } }		set_variable = { name = loans_var		value = 0 } }
	if	= { limit	= { NOT = { has_variable = temp_subtract } }	set_variable = { name = temp_subtract	value = 0 } }
	effect_loans_update = yes
}
effect_loans_update = {
	set_variable = {	# Sure you could do this more elegantly, but what's done is done.
		name = display_loans_var
		value = {
			value		= var:loans_var
			multiply	= 1000000	# 1M
		}
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = temp_subtract
		value = {
			value		= var:loans_var
			multiply	=-1000000	#-1M
		}
	}
}
effect_clear_loans_treasury = {
	add_treasury = var:temp_subtract
	set_variable = { name = loans_var		value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = temp_subtract	value = 0 }
}
effect_clear_loans_IVP = {
	add_investment_pool = var:temp_subtract
	set_variable = { name = loans_var		value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = temp_subtract	value = 0 }
}

effect_seed_setup = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = country_scope
	effect_instant_seed_autarky				= yes
	effect_seed_military_industry			= yes
	effect_seed_logging_private				= yes
	effect_seed_const						= yes
	effect_seed_infrastructure				= yes
	effect_make_admins						= yes
	effect_seed_resources_private			= yes
	effect_seed_resources					= yes
	# effect_fix_construction_goods_shortage	= yes
	# effect_build_wood						= yes
	# effect_build_fabric						= yes
	# effect_build_tools						= yes	# Breaks somewhere
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } trigger_microstate = no }
		}
		effect_seed_industry_private_state		= yes
		if = {
			limit = { trigger_greenhouse_allowed = yes trigger_inhospitable_terrain = no }
			effect_seed_greenhouses_private_state	= yes
		}
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			is_incorporated = no
			trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } trigger_microstate = no }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trigger_greenhouse_allowed = yes trigger_inhospitable_terrain = no }
			effect_seed_greenhouses_state	= yes
		}
	}
}
effect_kickstart_trade = {
	every_market = {
		limit = {
			OWNER = {
				NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_isolationism }
				total_population > 1000000	# 1M
				market_capital = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_building = building_trade_center }
						any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_trade_center level < 100 }
					}
				}
			}
			mg:merchant_marine = { market_goods_sell_orders > 10 }
		}
		OWNER.market_capital = { create_building = { building = building_trade_center	level = 100 } }
	}
}
effect_setup_replace_agro_state = {
	set_variable = {
		name = agro_target
		value = {
			value = 0
			if = { limit = { has_building = building_rye_farm }			add = b:building_rye_farm.level }
			if = { limit = { has_building = building_wheat_farm }		add = b:building_wheat_farm.level }
			if = { limit = { has_building = building_maize_farm }		add = b:building_maize_farm.level }
			if = { limit = { has_building = building_rice_farm }		add = b:building_rice_farm.level }
			if = { limit = { has_building = building_millet_farm }		add = b:building_millet_farm.level }
			if = { limit = { has_building = building_livestock_ranch }	add = b:building_livestock_ranch.level }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:agro_target > 0 }
		create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm		level = var:agro_target }
		create_building = { building = building_autarky_greenhouse	level = var:agro_target }
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_building = building_rye_farm
		remove_building = building_wheat_farm
		remove_building = building_maize_farm
		remove_building = building_rice_farm
		remove_building = building_millet_farm
		remove_building = building_livestock_ranch
	}
	remove_variable = agro_target
}

effect_buildings_setup = {
	every_state = {
		limit = {	# This is the reason it goes under GLOBAL rather than BUILDINGS (PMs and market conditions aren't initialised)
			OR = {
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_urban_center has_active_production_method = pm_gas_streetlights		}	# Place a synth oil plant in every state that has gas street lights at game start.
				AND = {
					trigger_capital_or_market_capital = yes
					OWNER = { country_rank >= rank_value:unrecognized_major_power }															# Plus, every major_power and above also gets one
					any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_urban_center has_active_production_method = pm_market_squares	}	# We need to ensure they have glass
				}
			}
		}
		create_building = { building = building_synthetic_oil_plants	level = 1 reserves = 1 activate_production_methods = { "pm_road_carts" "pm_no_charcoal" "pm_no_synth_rubber" "pm_coal_gasification" } }
	}
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			OWNER = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = percussion_cap } }
			OR = {
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_arms_industry }
				any_scope_building = { is_building_type = building_artillery_foundries }
			}
			market.mg:explosives = { market_goods_sell_orders < 50 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { market.mg:coal = { market_goods_sell_orders < 10 } }
			create_building = { building = building_charcoal_burner	level = 1 reserves = 1 activate_production_methods = { "pm_road_carts" "pm_automation_disabled" "pm_unfiltered" "pm_charcoal_kiln" } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = distillation } }
			activate_production_method = { building_type = building_charcoal_burner production_method = pm_dust_separation_charcoal }
			create_building = { building = building_synthetic_oil_plants	level = 1 reserves = 1 activate_production_methods = { "pm_road_carts" "pm_no_charcoal" "pm_no_synth_rubber" "pm_coal_gasification" } }
		}
		create_building = { building = building_chemical_plants				level = 1 reserves = 1 subsidized = yes activate_production_methods = { "pm_road_carts" "pm_automation_disabled" "pm_simple_fertilizers" } }
		create_building = { building = building_explosives_factory			level = 1 reserves = 1 subsidized = yes activate_production_methods = { "pm_road_carts" "pm_automation_disabled" "pm_early_soda" } }
	}
	s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL.region_state:BIC = {
		create_building = { building = building_tea_greenhouse		add_ownership = { company = { type = company_east_india_company country = c:GBR levels = 5 } } reserves = 1 }
		create_building = { building = building_tobacco_greenhouse	add_ownership = { company = { type = company_east_india_company country = c:GBR levels = 5 } } reserves = 1 }
		create_building = { building = building_opium_greenhouse	add_ownership = { company = { type = company_east_india_company country = c:GBR levels = 5 } } reserves = 1 }
		remove_building = building_tea_plantation
		remove_building = building_tobacco_plantation
		remove_building = building_opium_plantation
	}
	s:STATE_BOMBAY.region_state:BIC = {
		create_building = { building = building_tea_greenhouse		add_ownership = { company = { type = company_east_india_company country = c:GBR levels = 5 } } reserves = 1 }
		create_building = { building = building_tobacco_greenhouse	add_ownership = { company = { type = company_east_india_company country = c:GBR levels = 5 } } reserves = 1 }
		create_building = { building = building_opium_greenhouse	add_ownership = { company = { type = company_east_india_company country = c:GBR levels = 5 } } reserves = 1 }
		remove_building = building_tea_plantation
		remove_building = building_tobacco_plantation
		remove_building = building_opium_plantation
	}
	s:STATE_MADRAS.region_state:BIC = {
		create_building = { building = building_tea_greenhouse		add_ownership = { company = { type = company_east_india_company country = c:GBR levels = 5 } } reserves = 1 }
		create_building = { building = building_tobacco_greenhouse	add_ownership = { company = { type = company_east_india_company country = c:GBR levels = 5 } } reserves = 1 }
		create_building = { building = building_opium_greenhouse	add_ownership = { company = { type = company_east_india_company country = c:GBR levels = 5 } } reserves = 1 }
		remove_building = building_tea_plantation
		remove_building = building_tobacco_plantation
		remove_building = building_opium_plantation
	}
	every_state = {
		effect_setup_replace_agro_state		= yes
		effect_setup_replace_plant_state	= yes
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized } }
		if = {
			limit = { market.mg:iron = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 200 } }
			activate_production_method = { building_type = building_construction_sector production_method = pm_iron_frame_buildings }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { market.mg:tools = { market_goods_sell_orders >= 100 } }
			activate_production_method = { building_type = building_construction_sector production_method = pm_hardwood_frame_buildings }
		}
		effect_recalc_build_options			= yes
		effect_seed_setup					= yes
	}
	effect_kickstart_trade = yes
}
effect_historical_colony_vars = {
	random_state_region = { limit = { region = sr:region_senegal }			save_scope_as = senegal_region_scope }
	random_state_region = { limit = { region = sr:region_niger }			save_scope_as = niger_region_scope }
	random_state_region = { limit = { region = sr:region_ethiopia }			save_scope_as = ethiopia_region_scope }
	random_state_region = { limit = { region = sr:region_zanj }				save_scope_as = zanj_region_scope }
	random_state_region = { limit = { region = sr:region_southern_africa }	save_scope_as = southern_africa_region_scope }
	random_state_region = { limit = { region = sr:region_congo } save_scope_as = congo_region_scope }
	random_state_region = { limit = { region = sr:region_indonesia } save_scope_as = indonesia_region_scope }
	random_state_region = { limit = { region = sr:region_indochina } save_scope_as = indochina_region_scope }
	random_state_region = { limit = { region = sr:region_arabic } save_scope_as = arabic_region_scope }
	s:STATE_SOUTH_BENGAL = { save_scope_as = india_region_scope }
	s:STATE_BEIJING = { save_scope_as = china_region_scope }
	c:BIC = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = india_subject_var } }		set_variable = india_subject_var } }
	c:DEI = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = indonesia_subject_var } }	set_variable = indonesia_subject_var } }
	# c:PHI = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = indonesia_subject_var } }	set_variable = indonesia_subject_var } }	# Immediately becomes invalid when resetting subject variables
}
effect_general_setup = {
	effect_historical_obsessions = yes
	effect_buildings_setup = yes
	effect_historical_colony_vars = yes
	s:STATE_HAWAIIAN_ISLANDS		= { add_state_trait = state_trait_pearl_harbor }
	s:STATE_ERITREA					= { add_state_trait = state_trait_natural_harbors_djibouti }
	s:STATE_ANHALT					= { add_state_trait = state_trait_good_soils }
	s:STATE_BRUNSWICK				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_good_soils }
	s:STATE_RHINELAND				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_saar_coalfield }
	s:STATE_DISTRICT_OF_COLUMBIA	= { if = { limit = { NOT = { is_homeland = cu:yankee } } add_homeland = cu:yankee } }
	s:STATE_PALESTINE				= {
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_homeland = cu:sephardic } } add_homeland = cu:sephardic }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_homeland = cu:ashkenazi } } add_homeland = cu:ashkenazi }
	}
	s:STATE_HEREROLAND				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_namib_desert }
	s:STATE_NAMAQUALAND				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_namib_desert }
	s:STATE_SOUTH_ANGOLA			= { add_state_trait = state_trait_namib_desert }
	s:STATE_ALTO_PARAGUAY			= { remove_state_trait = state_trait_gran_chaco }
	effect_republican_paternalistic = yes
	# every_country = { IPB_non_monarchistizer_effect = yes }	# Removed - Reactionary means Reactionary.
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = empiricism }
		ig:ig_landowners = { if = { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_hierarchic } remove_ideology = ideology_hierarchic add_ideology = ideology_hierarchic_modern } }
	}
	every_country = { effect_reorder_landowner_traits = yes }
	every_state_region = {
		limit = {
			state_is_in_india = yes
			NOT = { is_homeland = cu:anglo_indian }
		}
		add_homeland = cu:anglo_indian
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_isolationism	# Intentionally not has_law_or_variant
			has_technology_researched = international_trade
			NOR = {
				THIS = c:CHI	# Great Qing
				THIS = c:JAP	# Japan
				THIS = c:KOR	# Korea
				THIS = c:SIA	# Siam
				THIS = c:NEP	# Nepal
				THIS = c:BHU	# Bhutan
				THIS = c:SIK	# Sikkim	(Himalayas)
				THIS = c:GLD	# Geledi	(Somaliland)
				THIS = c:KAU	# Kaurna	(Aboriginals)
				THIS = c:PRG	# Paraguay
			}
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_mercantilism
	}
	c:KAU = { activate_law = law_type:law_anarchy }
	c:BIC = {
		effect_add_anglo_indian_acceptance = yes	# TODO: Potentially makes the game too easy.
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = {
				culture = {
					has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
					NOT = { THIS ?= cu:anglo_indian }
				}
				OR = {
					pop_employment_building_group = bg_government
					is_pop_type = bureaucrats
					is_pop_type = clerks
				}
			}
			change_pop_culture = { target = cu:anglo_indian value = 1 }
		}
	}
	c:BIC = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = india_subject_var } }		set_variable = india_subject_var } }
	c:DEI = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = indonesia_subject_var } }	set_variable = indonesia_subject_var } }
	# c:PHI = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = indonesia_subject_var } }	set_variable = indonesia_subject_var } }
}
effect_claim_homelands = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT NOR = { OWNER = ROOT has_claim_by = ROOT } }
		state_region = { add_claim = ROOT }
	}
	every_state = {
		limit = {
			state_region = {
				NOT = { is_homeland = cu:north_german }
				OR = {
					is_homeland = cu:lithuanian
					is_homeland = cu:latvian
					is_homeland = cu:estonian
				}
			}
			NOR = {
				OWNER ?= c:UBD
				has_claim_by = c:UBD
			}
		}
		state_region = { add_claim = c:UBD }
	}
}

effect_take_homelands = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT NOT = { OWNER = ROOT } }
		set_state_owner = ROOT
	}
}
effect_unincorporate_non_homelands = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT } is_incorporated = yes }
		set_state_type = unincorporated
	}
}
effect_art_patronage = {
	if = {
		limit = { capital = { has_building = building_arts_academy } }
		if = {
			limit = { capital = { is_production_method_active = { building_type = building_arts_academy production_method = pm_traditional_patronage } } NOT = { has_modifier = modifier_traditional_patronage } }
			add_modifier	= modifier_traditional_patronage
			remove_modifier	= modifier_bourgeoisie_patronage
			remove_modifier	= modifier_independent_artists
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { capital = { is_production_method_active = { building_type = building_arts_academy production_method = pm_bourgeoisie_patronage } } NOT = { has_modifier = modifier_bourgeoisie_patronage } }
			add_modifier	= modifier_bourgeoisie_patronage
			remove_modifier	= modifier_traditional_patronage
			remove_modifier	= modifier_independent_artists
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { capital = { is_production_method_active = { building_type = building_arts_academy production_method = pm_independent_artists } } NOT = { has_modifier = modifier_independent_artists } }
			add_modifier	= modifier_independent_artists
			remove_modifier	= modifier_traditional_patronage
			remove_modifier	= modifier_bourgeoisie_patronage
		}
	}
	else = {
		while = { limit = { has_modifier = modifier_traditional_patronage }	remove_modifier	= modifier_traditional_patronage }
		while = { limit = { has_modifier = modifier_bourgeoisie_patronage }	remove_modifier	= modifier_bourgeoisie_patronage }
		while = { limit = { has_modifier = modifier_independent_artists }	remove_modifier	= modifier_independent_artists }
	}
}

	# while = { limit = { has_modifier = modifier_bourgeoisie_patronage }	remove_modifier	= modifier_bourgeoisie_patronage }
	# while = { limit = { has_modifier = modifier_independent_artists }	remove_modifier	= modifier_independent_artists }
# }
effect_remove_subject_var = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = india_subject_var
			any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian NOT = { THIS ?= cu:anglo_indian } }
		}
		custom_label = effect_remove_subject_var.tt
		remove_variable = india_subject_var
		if = {
			limit = { any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:anglo_indian } }
			remove_primary_culture = cu:anglo_indian
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_modifier = anglo_indian_cultural_acceptance_modifier_negative }
			remove_modifier = anglo_indian_cultural_acceptance_modifier_negative
		}
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = indonesia_subject_var
			any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_southeast_asian }
		}
		custom_label = effect_remove_subject_var.tt
		remove_variable = indonesia_subject_var
	}
}
effect_reset_autarky_pm_state = {
	OWNER = {
		switch = {
			trigger = has_law
			law_type:law_serfdom = {
				PREV = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { is_production_method_active	= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_serfdom } } }
						activate_production_method						= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_serfdom }
					}
				}
			}
			law_type:law_manorialism = {
				PREV = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { is_production_method_active	= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_corvee_labor } } }
						activate_production_method						= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_corvee_labor }
					}
				}
			}
			law_type:law_homesteading = {
				PREV = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { is_production_method_active	= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_homesteading_building_subsistence } } }
						activate_production_method						= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_homesteading_building_subsistence }
					}
				}
			}
			fallback = {
				PREV = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { is_production_method_active	= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_serfdom_no } } }
						activate_production_method						= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_serfdom_no }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_reset_autarky_pm = {
	switch = {
		trigger = has_law
		law_type:law_serfdom = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_production_method_active	= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_serfdom } } }
				activate_production_method						= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_serfdom }
			}
		}
		law_type:law_manorialism = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_production_method_active	= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_corvee_labor } } }
				activate_production_method						= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_corvee_labor }
			}
		}
		law_type:law_homesteading = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_production_method_active	= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_homesteading_building_subsistence } } }
				activate_production_method						= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_homesteading_building_subsistence }
			}
		}
		fallback = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_production_method_active	= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_serfdom_no } } }
				activate_production_method						= { building_type = building_autarky_farm	production_method = pm_serfdom_no }
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_instant_seed_autarky_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_autarky_farm } }
		create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels = 1 } } }
		OWNER = {
			if = { limit = { has_variable = loans_var } change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.2 } }	# Every building costs 200 points x 1k/point in Millions
			else = { add_treasury =-200000 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_autarky_greenhouse } }
		create_building = { building = building_autarky_greenhouse	add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels = 1 } } }
		OWNER = {
			if = { limit = { has_variable = loans_var } change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.4 } }	# Every building costs 400 points x 1k/point in Millions
			else = { add_treasury =-400000 }
		}
	}
}
effect_instant_seed_autarky = {
	every_scope_state = { effect_instant_seed_autarky_state = yes }
}
effect_instant_seed_autarky_ivr = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = country_scope
	# every_scope_state = {
		# limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_autarky_farm } }
		# create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels = 1 } } }
		# ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.2 } }	# Every building costs 200 points x 1k/point in Millions
	# }
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				THIS = ROOT
				trigger_PREV_has_investment_rights = yes
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = { effect_instant_seed_autarky_state = yes }
		hidden_effect = { effect_reset_autarky_pm = yes }
		hidden_effect = { activate_production_method = { building_type = building_autarky_greenhouse		production_method = pm_tools_disabled_subsistence } }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = effect_tool_fertilizer_switch.tt
			effect_fertilizer_switch = yes
			effect_tools_switch = yes
		}
	}
}
effect_instant_build_autarky_ivr = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = country_scope
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				THIS = ROOT
				trigger_PREV_has_investment_rights = yes
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = { effect_delete_finite_agro_state = yes }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { b:building_autarky_farm.level < arable_land }
			set_variable = { name = old_level value = b:building_autarky_farm.level }
			if		= { limit = { arable_land <=  11 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   10 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  21 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   20 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  31 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   30 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  41 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   40 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  51 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   50 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  61 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   60 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  71 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   70 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  81 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   80 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  91 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   90 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  101 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =  100 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  201 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =  200 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  301 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =  300 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  401 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =  400 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  501 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =  500 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  601 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =  600 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  701 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =  700 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  801 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =  800 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  901 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =  900 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <= 1001 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm			add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels = 1000 } } } }
			if = {
				limit = { b:building_autarky_farm.level > var:old_level }	# Required to prevent division by zero errors
				set_variable = {
					name = temp_price
					value = {
						value		= b:building_autarky_farm.level
						subtract	= var:old_level
						multiply = {
							value	= construction_cost_low			# Building price in construction points
							divide	= 1000							# 1k/point in Millions
						}
						min			= 0
					}
				}
				ROOT = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = PREV.var:temp_price } }
			}
			remove_variable = old_level
			remove_variable = temp_price
		}
	}
}
effect_instant_build_infres_autarky_ivr = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = country_scope
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				THIS = ROOT
				trigger_PREV_has_investment_rights = yes
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { b:building_autarky_greenhouse.level < arable_land }
			set_variable = { name = old_level value = b:building_autarky_greenhouse.level }
			if		= { limit = { arable_land <=  11 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_greenhouse	add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   10 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  21 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_greenhouse	add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   20 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  31 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_greenhouse	add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   30 } } } }

			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  801 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_greenhouse	add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =  800 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <=  901 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_greenhouse	add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =  900 } } } }
			else_if	= { limit = { arable_land <= 1001 }	create_building = { building = building_autarky_greenhouse	add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels = 1000 } } } }
			if = {

			remove_variable = temp_price
		}

effect_instant_seed_greenhouses = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			is_treaty_port = no
			trigger_inhospitable_terrain = no
			trigger_if = { limit = { is_capital = no } NOT = { trigger_microstate = yes } }
		}
		effect_instant_seed_greenhouses_state = yes
	}
}

effect_set_culture_scopes_state = {	# TODO: Rewrite for state scope
	save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope
	OWNER = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european has_homeland = scope:state_scope } }
			ordered_primary_culture = { limit = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european has_homeland = scope:state_scope }			position = 0 save_scope_as = european_culture		}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european } }
			ordered_primary_culture = { limit = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european }											position = 0 save_scope_as = european_culture		}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { any_scope_pop = { culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european } } }
			ordered_scope_pop = {
				limit = { culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european } }
				order_by = total_size
				position = 0
				culture = { save_scope_as = european_culture }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_east_asian has_homeland = scope:state_scope } }
			ordered_primary_culture = { limit = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_east_asian has_homeland = scope:state_scope }			position = 0 save_scope_as = east_asian_culture		}
		}

effect_assimilate_pops_state = {
	effect_set_culture_scopes_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:european_culture }
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = { culture = { trigger_culture_can_assimilate = { trait_group = heritage_group_european } NOT = { THIS ?= scope:european_culture } has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european } }
			change_pop_culture = { target = scope:european_culture		value = 1.00 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:european_culture }
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = { culture = { NOR = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT has_homeland = PREV.STATE } NOT = { THIS ?= scope:european_culture } has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european } }
			change_pop_culture = { target = scope:european_culture		value = 1.00 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:east_asian_culture }
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = { culture = { trigger_culture_can_assimilate = { trait_group = heritage_group_east_asian } NOT = { THIS ?= scope:east_asian_culture } has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_east_asian } }
			change_pop_culture = { target = scope:east_asian_culture	value = 1.00 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:east_asian_culture }
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = { culture = { NOR = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT has_homeland = PREV.STATE } NOT = { THIS ?= scope:east_asian_culture } has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_east_asian } }
			change_pop_culture = { target = scope:east_asian_culture	value = 1.00 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:north_asian_culture }
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = { culture = { trigger_culture_can_assimilate = { trait_group = heritage_group_north_asian } NOT = { THIS ?= scope:north_asian_culture } has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_north_asian } }
			change_pop_culture = { target = scope:north_asian_culture	value = 1.00 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:north_asian_culture }
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = { culture = { NOR = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT has_homeland = PREV.STATE } NOT = { THIS ?= scope:north_asian_culture } has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_north_asian } }
			change_pop_culture = { target = scope:north_asian_culture	value = 1.00 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:central_asian_culture }
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = { culture = { trigger_culture_can_assimilate = { trait_group = heritage_group_central_asian } NOT = { THIS ?= scope:central_asian_culture } has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_central_asian } }
			change_pop_culture = { target = scope:central_asian_culture	value = 1.00 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:central_asian_culture }
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = { culture = { NOR = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT has_homeland = PREV.STATE } NOT = { THIS ?= scope:central_asian_culture } has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_central_asian } }
			change_pop_culture = { target = scope:central_asian_culture	value = 1.00 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:indian_culture }
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = { culture = { trigger_culture_can_assimilate = { trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian } NOT = { THIS ?= scope:indian_culture } has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian } }
			change_pop_culture = { target = scope:indian_culture		value = 1.00 }
		}

effect_purveyor_of_progres_railway = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = country_scope
	if = {
		limit = { has_company = company_type:company_great_indian_railway }
		every_state = {
			limit = {	# Delhi-Calcutta line
				NOT = { has_building = building_railway }
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
					state_region = s:STATE_BIHAR
					state_region = s:STATE_AWADH
					state_region = s:STATE_AGRA
					state_region = s:STATE_DELHI
				}
			}
			create_building = { building = "building_railway" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { company = { type = company_great_indian_railway country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
			scope:country_scope = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.8 } }	# Every building costs 800 points x 1k/point in Millions
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {	# Allahabad-Bombay line
				NOT = { has_building = building_railway }
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_AGRA
					state_region = s:STATE_BUNDELKHAND
					state_region = s:STATE_CENTRAL_PROVINCES
					state_region = s:STATE_MALWA
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
				}
			}
			create_building = { building = "building_railway" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { company = { type = company_great_indian_railway country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
			scope:country_scope = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.8 } }	# Every building costs 800 points x 1k/point in Millions
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {	# Bombay-Madras line
				NOT = { has_building = building_railway }
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
					state_region = s:STATE_HYDERABAD
					state_region = s:STATE_KURNOOL
					state_region = s:STATE_MADRAS
				}
			}
			create_building = { building = "building_railway" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { company = { type = company_great_indian_railway country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
			scope:country_scope = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.8 } }	# Every building costs 800 points x 1k/point in Millions
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {	# Gujarat-Bombay line
				NOT = { has_building = building_railway }
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
					state_region = s:STATE_GUJARAT
				}
			}
			create_building = { building = "building_railway" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { company = { type = company_great_indian_railway country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
			scope:country_scope = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.8 } }	# Every building costs 800 points x 1k/point in Millions
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { can_establish_company = yes }
		add_company = company_type:company_great_indian_railway
		company:company_great_indian_railway = { set_company_state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY }
		every_state = {
			limit = {	# Delhi-Calcutta line
				NOT = { has_building = building_railway }
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
					state_region = s:STATE_BIHAR
					state_region = s:STATE_AWADH
					state_region = s:STATE_AGRA
					state_region = s:STATE_DELHI
				}
			}
			create_building = { building = "building_railway" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { company = { type = company_great_indian_railway country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
			scope:country_scope = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.8 } }	# Every building costs 800 points x 1k/point in Millions
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {	# Allahabad-Bombay line
				NOT = { has_building = building_railway }
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_AGRA
					state_region = s:STATE_BUNDELKHAND
					state_region = s:STATE_CENTRAL_PROVINCES
					state_region = s:STATE_MALWA
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
				}
			}
			create_building = { building = "building_railway" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { company = { type = company_great_indian_railway country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
			scope:country_scope = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.8 } }	# Every building costs 800 points x 1k/point in Millions
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {	# Bombay-Madras line
				NOT = { has_building = building_railway }
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
					state_region = s:STATE_HYDERABAD
					state_region = s:STATE_KURNOOL
					state_region = s:STATE_MADRAS
				}
			}
			create_building = { building = "building_railway" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { company = { type = company_great_indian_railway country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
			scope:country_scope = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.8 } }	# Every building costs 800 points x 1k/point in Millions
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {	# Gujarat-Bombay line
				NOT = { has_building = building_railway }
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
					state_region = s:STATE_GUJARAT
				}
			}
			create_building = { building = "building_railway" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { company = { type = company_great_indian_railway country = "scope:country_scope" levels = 1 } } }
			scope:country_scope = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.8 } }	# Every building costs 800 points x 1k/point in Millions
		}
	}
	else = {
		every_state = {
			limit = {	# Delhi-Calcutta line
				NOT = { has_building = building_railway }
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
					state_region = s:STATE_BIHAR
					state_region = s:STATE_AWADH
					state_region = s:STATE_AGRA
					state_region = s:STATE_DELHI
				}
			}
			create_building = { building = "building_railway" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   1 } } }
			scope:country_scope = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.8 } }	# Every building costs 800 points x 1k/point in Millions
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {	# Allahabad-Bombay line
				NOT = { has_building = building_railway }
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_AGRA

					state_region = s:STATE_CENTRAL_PROVINCES
					state_region = s:STATE_MALWA
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
				}
			}
			create_building = { building = "building_railway" subsidized = yes add_ownership = { country = { country = scope:country_scope levels =   1 } } }
			scope:country_scope = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.8 } }	# Every building costs 800 points x 1k/point in Millions
		}

				NOT = { has_building = building_railway }
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
					state_region = s:STATE_HYDERABAD
					state_region = s:STATE_KURNOOL

			scope:country_scope = { change_variable = { name = loans_var add = 0.8 } }	# Every building costs 800 points x 1k/point in Millions

effect_claim_africa = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_country = { target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes has_variable = senegal_subject_var } }
		random_country = {
			limit = { target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes has_variable = senegal_subject_var }
			save_temporary_scope_as = senegal_subject
		}
		every_state = { limit = { NOR = { OWNER = scope:senegal_subject has_claim_by = scope:senegal_subject } is_largest_state_in_region = yes	region = sr:region_senegal }			state_region = { add_claim = scope:senegal_subject } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_country = { target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes has_variable = niger_subject_var } }
		random_country = {
			limit = { target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes has_variable = niger_subject_var }
			save_temporary_scope_as = niger_subject
		}
		every_state = { limit = { NOR = { OWNER = scope:niger_subject has_claim_by = scope:niger_subject } is_largest_state_in_region = yes		region = sr:region_niger }				state_region = { add_claim = scope:niger_subject } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_country = { target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes has_variable = congo_subject_var } }
		random_country = {
			limit = { target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes has_variable = congo_subject_var }
			save_temporary_scope_as = congo_subject
		}
		every_state = { limit = { NOR = { OWNER = scope:congo_subject has_claim_by = scope:congo_subject } is_largest_state_in_region = yes		region = sr:region_congo }				state_region = { add_claim = scope:congo_subject } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_country = { target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes has_variable = southern_africa_subject_var } }
		random_country = {
			limit = { target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes has_variable = southern_africa_subject_var }
			save_temporary_scope_as = saf_subject
		}
		every_state = { limit = { NOR = { OWNER = scope:saf_subject has_claim_by = scope:saf_subject } is_largest_state_in_region = yes			region = sr:region_southern_africa }	state_region = { add_claim = scope:saf_subject } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_country = { target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes has_variable = zanj_subject_var } }
		random_country = {
			limit = { target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes has_variable = zanj_subject_var }
			save_temporary_scope_as = zanj_subject
		}
		every_state = { limit = { NOR = { OWNER = scope:zanj_subject has_claim_by = scope:zanj_subject } is_largest_state_in_region = yes		region = sr:region_zanj }				state_region = { add_claim = scope:zanj_subject } }
	}

effect_historical_obsessions = {
	if = { limit = { cu:russian			= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = liquor } } }			cu:russian			= { add_cultural_obsession		= liquor } }
	if = { limit = { cu:finnish			= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = liquor } } }			cu:finnish			= { add_cultural_obsession		= liquor } }
	if = { limit = { cu:finnish			= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = coffee } } }			cu:finnish			= { add_cultural_obsession		= coffee } }
	if = { limit = { cu:yankee			= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = sugar } } }			cu:yankee			= { add_cultural_obsession		= sugar } }
	if = { limit = { cu:yankee			= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = small_arms } } }		cu:yankee			= { add_cultural_obsession		= small_arms } }
	if = { limit = { cu:yankee			= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = oil } } }				cu:yankee			= { add_cultural_obsession		= oil } }
	if = { limit = { cu:dixie			= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = sugar } } }			cu:dixie			= { add_cultural_obsession		= sugar } }
	if = { limit = { cu:dixie			= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = small_arms } } }		cu:dixie			= { add_cultural_obsession		= small_arms } }
	if = { limit = { cu:dixie			= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = oil } } }				cu:dixie			= { add_cultural_obsession		= oil } }
	if = { limit = { cu:afro_american	= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = sugar } } }			cu:afro_american	= { add_cultural_obsession		= sugar } }
	if = { limit = { cu:afro_american	= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = small_arms } } }		cu:afro_american	= { add_cultural_obsession		= small_arms } }
	if = { limit = { cu:hawaiian		= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = sugar } } }			cu:hawaiian			= { add_cultural_obsession		= sugar } }
	if = { limit = { cu:hawaiian		= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = small_arms } } }		cu:hawaiian			= { add_cultural_obsession		= small_arms } }
	if = { limit = { cu:platinean		= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = meat } } }				cu:platinean		= { add_cultural_obsession		= meat } }
	if = { limit = { cu:argentine		= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = meat } } }				cu:argentine		= { add_cultural_obsession		= meat } }
	if = { limit = { cu:uruguayan		= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = meat } } }				cu:uruguayan		= { add_cultural_obsession		= meat } }
	if = { limit = { cu:paraguayan		= { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = meat } } }				cu:paraguayan		= { add_cultural_obsession		= meat } }

effect_populate_state = {	# state scope
	save_temporary_scope_as = state_to_populate
	OWNER = {
		ordered_scope_state = {
			limit = { state_unemployment_rate > 0 NOT = { THIS ?= scope:state_to_populate } }
			order_by = state_unemployment_rate
			position = 0
			if = {
				limit = { any_scope_pop = { is_employed = no has_state_religion = yes pop_acceptance >= 60 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } } }
				ordered_scope_pop = {
					limit = { is_employed = no has_state_religion = yes pop_acceptance >= 60 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } }
					order_by = total_size
					position = 0
					# save_temporary_scope_as = pop_to_move
					# move_partial_pop = {
						# state = scope:state_to_populate
						# population = {
							# value = 400000
							# max = scope:pop_to_move.total_size
						# }
					# }
					move_pop = scope:state_to_populate
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = peasants has_state_religion = yes pop_acceptance >= 60 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } } }
				ordered_scope_pop = {
					limit = { is_pop_type = peasants has_state_religion = yes pop_acceptance >= 60 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } }
					order_by = total_size
					position = 0
					save_temporary_scope_as = pop_to_move
					move_partial_pop = {
						state = scope:state_to_populate
						population = {
							value = 400000
							max = scope:pop_to_move.total_size
						}
					}

				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { any_scope_pop = { is_employed = no pop_acceptance >= 60 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } } }
				ordered_scope_pop = {
					limit = { is_employed = no pop_acceptance >= 60 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } }
					order_by = total_size
					position = 0
					# save_temporary_scope_as = pop_to_move
					# move_partial_pop = {
						# state = scope:state_to_populate
						# population = {
							# value = 400000
							# max = scope:pop_to_move.total_size
						# }
					# }
					move_pop = scope:state_to_populate
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = peasants pop_acceptance >= 60 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } } }
				ordered_scope_pop = {
					limit = { is_pop_type = peasants pop_acceptance >= 60 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } }
					order_by = total_size
					position = 0
					save_temporary_scope_as = pop_to_move
					move_partial_pop = {
						state = scope:state_to_populate
						population = {
							value = 400000
							max = scope:pop_to_move.total_size
						}
					}

				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { any_scope_pop = { is_employed = no pop_acceptance >= 40 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } } }
				ordered_scope_pop = {
					limit = { is_employed = no pop_acceptance >= 40 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } }
					order_by = total_size
					position = 0
					# save_temporary_scope_as = pop_to_move
					# move_partial_pop = {
						# state = scope:state_to_populate
						# population = {
							# value = 400000
							# max = scope:pop_to_move.total_size
						# }
					# }
					move_pop = scope:state_to_populate
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = peasants pop_acceptance >= 40 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } } }
				ordered_scope_pop = {
					limit = { is_pop_type = peasants pop_acceptance >= 40 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } }
					order_by = total_size
					position = 0
					save_temporary_scope_as = pop_to_move
					move_partial_pop = {
						state = scope:state_to_populate
						population = {
							value = 400000
							max = scope:pop_to_move.total_size
						}
					}

				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { any_scope_pop = { is_employed = no pop_acceptance >= 20 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } } }
				ordered_scope_pop = {
					limit = { is_employed = no pop_acceptance >= 20 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } }
					order_by = total_size
					position = 0
					# save_temporary_scope_as = pop_to_move

					# }

				}
			}

				ordered_scope_pop = {
					limit = { is_pop_type = peasants pop_acceptance >= 20 NOT = { STATE ?= scope:state_to_populate } }
					order_by = total_size
					position = 0

effect_convert_largest_unemployed_pop_to_peasants = {
	ordered_scope_pop = {
		limit = { is_employed = no NOT = { is_pop_type = peasants } }
		order_by = total_size
		position = 0
		change_poptype = pop_type:peasants
	}
}
effect_build_naval_base_extensions = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { is_coastal = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { naval_extensions_to_build > 0 }
			while = {
				count = naval_extensions_to_build
				start_building_construction = building_infres_naval_base
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_add_ally_war_support = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		add_diplomatic_play_war_support = { target = ROOT value = 200 }
		if = {
			limit = { trigger_ROOT_initiator = yes }
			add_diplomatic_play_war_support = { target = initiator value = 200 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { trigger_ROOT_target = yes }
			add_diplomatic_play_war_support = { target = target value = 200 }
		}
	}
}
effect_subtract_enemy_war_support_limited = {
	# every_country = {
		# limit = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT }
		# every_diplomatic_play = {
			# limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
			# add_diplomatic_play_war_support = { target = PREV value = -100 }
		# }
	# }
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT }
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			scope:play_scope = {
				add_diplomatic_play_war_support = { target = scope:enemy_scope value =-100 }
			}
		}
	}
}

		# }

	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT }
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			scope:play_scope = {
				add_diplomatic_play_war_support = { target = scope:enemy_scope value =-200 }
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_add_war_reps = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT is_subject = no NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized } }
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
				name = war_reparations_amount
				value = {
					value = income
					multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
				}
			}
			scope:play_scope = {
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = ROOT
					target_country = scope:enemy_scope
					type = enforce_treaty_article
					article = {
						article = money_transfer
						source_country = scope:enemy_scope	# source is the country commiting to the article
						target_country = ROOT
						inputs = { { quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount } }
					}
					primary_demand = no
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_enforce_state_atheism = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = { is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = ROOT } NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_state_atheism } }
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			scope:play_scope = {
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = ROOT
					target_country = scope:enemy_scope
					type = enforce_treaty_article
					article = {
						article = law_commitment
						inputs = { { law_type = law_type:law_state_atheism } }
						source_country = scope:enemy_scope	# source is the country commiting to the article
						target_country = ROOT
					}
					primary_demand = no
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = academia } }		add_technology_researched = academia	}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = romanticism } }	add_technology_researched = romanticism	}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = empiricism } }		add_technology_researched = empiricism	}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_technology_researched = realism } }		add_technology_researched = realism		}
		}
	}
}

effect_add_subjugation = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				is_subject = no
				country_rank < rank_value:unrecognized_major_power
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_tributary		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_dominion		target = PREV }
						play_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
						war_participant_has_war_goal_of_type_against	= { type = make_protectorate	target = PREV }
					}
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
			scope:play_scope = {
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { is_country_type = unrecognized } }
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = ROOT
						type = make_tributary
						target_country = scope:enemy_scope
						primary_demand = no
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { is_country_type = colonial }
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = ROOT
						type = make_dominion
						target_country = scope:enemy_scope
						primary_demand = no
					}
				}
				else = {
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = ROOT
						type = make_protectorate
						target_country = scope:enemy_scope
						primary_demand = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_revoke_enemy_claims_in_play = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				save_temporary_scope_as = enemy_scope
				ROOT = { any_scope_state = { has_claim_by = scope:enemy_scope } }
			}
			ROOT = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = { has_claim_by = scope:enemy_scope }
					save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope
					scope:play_scope = {
						add_war_goal = {
							holder = ROOT
							type = revoke_claim
							target_state = scope:state_scope
							target_country = scope:enemy_scope
							primary_demand = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_transfer_homeland_in_play = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT
						has_claim_by = ROOT
						trigger_should_claim = { claimant = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT
						has_claim_by = ROOT
						trigger_should_claim = { claimant = ROOT }
					}
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope
				scope:play_scope = {
					if = {
						limit = { scope:state_scope = { has_claim_by = ROOT } }
						add_war_goal = {
							holder = ROOT
							type = return_state
							target_state = scope:state_scope
							primary_demand = yes
						}
					}
					else = {
						add_war_goal = {
							holder = ROOT
							type = conquer_state
							target_state = scope:state_scope
							primary_demand = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_transfer_homeland_in_play_to_ally = {
	every_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = { trigger_ROOT_committed_participant = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = play_scope
		every_scope_play_involved = {
			limit = { is_diplomatic_play_ally_of = ROOT }
			save_temporary_scope_as = ally_scope
			scope:play_scope = {	# Needs to be executed for every ally country, but in play_scope
				every_scope_play_involved = {
					limit = {
						is_diplomatic_play_enemy_of = ROOT	# Could theoretically also use ally_scope here, but allegiances can shift during the play
						any_scope_state = { trigger_ally_state = yes }
					}
					every_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								trigger_if		= { limit = { NOT = { is_country_type = colonial } }												is_homeland_of_country_cultures	= ROOT }
								trigger_if		= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { NAND = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial } } }	has_claim_by					= ROOT }
							}
							OR = {
								trigger_if		= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { THIS ?= c:CHI } }																				trigger_chinese_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:japanese } NOT = { is_country_type = colonial } } }			region = sr:region_japan }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial has_variable = senegal_subject_var } }			region = sr:region_senegal }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial has_variable = niger_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_niger }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial has_variable = ethiopia_subject_var } }			region = sr:region_ethiopia }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial has_variable = zanj_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_zanj }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial has_variable = southern_africa_subject_var } }	region = sr:region_southern_africa }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial has_variable = congo_subject_var } }				region = sr:region_congo }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial has_variable = indonesia_subject_var } }			region = sr:region_indonesia }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial has_variable = indochina_subject_var } }			region = sr:region_indochina }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial has_variable = arabic_subject_var } }			region = sr:region_arabic }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial has_variable = india_subject_var } }				state_is_in_india = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { is_subject_of = ROOT is_country_type = colonial has_variable = china_subject_var } }				trigger_chinese_state = yes }
								trigger_else_if	= { limit = { scope:ally_scope = { NOT = { is_country_type = colonial } } } is_homeland_of_country_cultures = scope:ally_scope }
								has_claim_by = scope:ally_scope
							}
						}
						save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope
						scope:play_scope = {	# Needs to be executed for every state, but in play_scope
							if = {
								limit = { scope:state_scope = { has_claim_by = scope:ally_scope } }
								add_war_goal = {
									holder = scope:ally_scope
									type = return_state
									target_state = scope:state_scope
									primary_demand = yes
								}
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { scope:state_scope = { is_treaty_port = yes } }
								add_war_goal = {
									holder = scope:ally_scope
									type = take_treaty_port
									target_state = scope:state_scope
									primary_demand = yes
								}
							}
							else = {
								add_war_goal = {
									holder = scope:ally_scope
									type = conquer_state
									target_state = scope:state_scope
									primary_demand = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

effect_reset_cultural_acceptance = {	# TODO: Rewrite to accept arguments?
	ordered_scope_pop = {
		limit = {
			state = { save_temporary_scope_as = state_scope }
			culture = {
				NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT }
				scope:state_scope = { "cultural_acceptance_delta(PREV)" < 0 }
				save_scope_as = culture_scope
			}
		}
		order_by = "cultural_acceptance_delta(culture)"
		culture = { save_scope_as = culture_scope }
	}
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { any_scope_pop = { culture = { THIS ?= scope:culture_scope } } }
		add_acceptance = {
			target = scope:culture_scope
			value = {
				add = 100
				max = {
					add = "cultural_acceptance_delta(scope:culture_scope)"
					multiply = -1 # cannot add more delta than what gets you to 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
