		limit = {
			OWNER = {
				NOR = {
					THIS ?= ROOT
					# has_variable = indochina_subject_var
				}
				target_is_our_or_fellow_subject = yes
			}
			region = sr:region_indochina 
		}
		set_state_owner = ROOT
	}
}
effect_add_anglo_indian_acceptance = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:british }
			NOT = { any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:anglo_indian } }
		}
		add_primary_culture = cu:anglo_indian
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:british } }
			any_primary_culture = { count > 1 }
		}
		remove_primary_culture = cu:anglo_indian
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = anglo_indian_cultural_acceptance_modifier_negative }
		remove_modifier = anglo_indian_cultural_acceptance_modifier_negative
	}
	hidden_effect = { set_local_variable = { name = acceptance_multiplier value = pop_acceptance_muliplier_shared_language } }	# Would otherwise use the value of the executing scope.
	if = {
		limit = { any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:british } has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_multicultural }
		add_culture_acceptance_modifier = {
			culture = cu:anglo_indian
			multiplier = local_var:acceptance_multiplier
			days = long_modifier_time	# 10 years
			is_decaying = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_primary_culture = { THIS ?= cu:british } }
		add_culture_acceptance_modifier = {
			culture = cu:anglo_indian
			multiplier = local_var:acceptance_multiplier
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = { remove_local_variable = acceptance_multiplier }
}
effect_reset_variables = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = worldwide_building_options }	remove_variable = worldwide_building_options }	# Important anti-lag measure
	if = { limit = { has_variable = industry_queueing }				remove_variable = industry_queueing }			# Important anti-lag measure
	if = { limit = { has_variable = greenhouse_queueing }			remove_variable = greenhouse_queueing }			# Important anti-lag measure
	if = { limit = { has_variable = inf_res_queueing }				remove_variable = inf_res_queueing }			# Important anti-lag measure
	if = { limit = { has_variable = 05_queueing }					remove_variable = 05_queueing }					# Important anti-lag measure
	if = { limit = { has_variable = 25_queueing }					remove_variable = 25_queueing }					# Important anti-lag measure
	if = { limit = { has_variable = 50_queueing }					remove_variable = 50_queueing }					# Important anti-lag measure
	if = { limit = { has_variable = max_queueing }					remove_variable = max_queueing }				# Important anti-lag measure
	if = { limit = { has_variable = goods_balancing }				remove_variable = goods_balancing }				# Important anti-lag measure
	if = { limit = { has_variable = arable_land_cheats }			remove_variable = arable_land_cheats }			# QoL, so I don't have to constantly go back in the menu.
	if = { limit = { has_variable = instant_building_options }		remove_variable = instant_building_options }	# QoL, so I don't have to constantly go back in the menu.
	if = { limit = { has_variable = ig_ideo_options }				remove_variable = ig_ideo_options }				# QoL, so I don't have to constantly go back in the menu.
	if = { limit = { has_variable = char_ideo_options }				remove_variable = char_ideo_options }			# QoL, so I don't have to constantly go back in the menu.
	if = { limit = { has_variable = party_options }					remove_variable = party_options }				# QoL, so I don't have to constantly go back in the menu.
	if = { limit = { has_variable = peasant_spawning }				remove_variable = peasant_spawning }			# QoL, so I don't have to constantly go back in the menu.
	if = { limit = { has_variable = unconditional_pop_spawning }	remove_variable = unconditional_pop_spawning }	# QoL, so I don't have to constantly go back in the menu.
	if = { limit = { has_variable = laggy_pop_options }				remove_variable = laggy_pop_options }			# QoL, so I don't have to constantly go back in the menu.
	if = { limit = { has_variable = pop_moving }					remove_variable = pop_moving }					# QoL, so I don't have to constantly go back in the menu.
}
# effect_create_agitators_void = {

effect_replace_reactionary_leaders = {
	every_interest_group = {
		limit = {
			leader ?= {
				OR = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_ethno_nationalist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_traditionalist_minoritarian
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_theocrat
				}
			}
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = ig_scope
		OWNER = {
			create_character = {
				female = yes
				age = 18
				ig_leader = yes
				# religion = rel:atheist
				interest_group = scope:ig_scope
				trait_generation = {
					add_trait = innovative
					if = {
						limit = { trait_value > 0 }
						set_variable = has_historical_traits
						add_experience = trait_value
					}
					else = { add_experience = 4 }
					add_random_trait = personality
					if = { limit = { has_trait = bigoted } remove_trait = bigoted add_random_trait = personality }
					if = {
						limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = has_historical_traits } trait_value <= 3 } }
						add_random_trait = skill
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_trait = traditionalist_commander }
						remove_trait = traditionalist_commander
					}
					if = { limit = { NOT = { has_modifier = modifier_character_extra_ten_percent_command } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_character_extra_ten_percent_command } }
				}
				on_created = {
					if = { limit = { NOT = { is_interest_group_type = ig_devout } } change_character_religion = rel:atheist }
					add_modifier = { name = modifier_character_popular_adored }
					if		= { limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = feminism has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy } }	set_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian_royalist }
					else_if	= { limit = { OWNER = { has_technology_researched = feminism } }											set_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian }
					else	= {																											set_ideology = ideology:ideology_utopian }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
effect_rebuild = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = $building$ } }
		save_temporary_scope_value_as = { name = $building$_level value = b:$building$.level }
		OWNER = { save_temporary_scope_as = owner_scope }
		remove_building = $building$
		create_building = { building = $building$ level = scope:$building$_level }
		# create_building = { building = $building$ add_ownership = { building = { type = building_financial_district country = "scope:owner_scope" levels = scope:$building$_level } } }
	}
}

effect_rebuild_capped = {
	every_state = {
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_autarky_farm }			remove_building = building_autarky_farm			create_building = { building = building_autarky_farm		level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_logging_camp }			remove_building = building_logging_camp			create_building = { building = building_logging_camp		level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_rubber_plantation }	remove_building = building_rubber_plantation	create_building = { building = building_rubber_plantation	level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_coal_mine }			remove_building = building_coal_mine			create_building = { building = building_coal_mine			level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_iron_mine }			remove_building = building_iron_mine			create_building = { building = building_iron_mine			level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_lead_mine }			remove_building = building_lead_mine			create_building = { building = building_lead_mine			level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_sulfur_mine }			remove_building = building_sulfur_mine			create_building = { building = building_sulfur_mine			level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_gold_mine }			remove_building = building_gold_mine			create_building = { building = building_gold_mine			level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_oil_rig }				remove_building = building_oil_rig				create_building = { building = building_oil_rig				level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_fishing_wharf }		remove_building = building_fishing_wharf		create_building = { building = building_fishing_wharf		level = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_building = building_whaling_station }		remove_building = building_whaling_station		create_building = { building = building_whaling_station		level = 1 } }
	}
}

effect_destroy_building = {
	show_as_tooltip	= { if = { limit = { any_scope_building = { is_building_type = $building$ } }	remove_building = $building$ } }
	hidden_effect	= {																				remove_building = $building$ }
}
effect_decentralize_depopulated_countries = {
	every_country = {
		limit = { total_population < 10000 }
		if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } make_independent = yes }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized } } set_country_type = decentralized }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { is_country_type = decentralized }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_isolationism } } activate_law = law_type:law_isolationism }
		every_scope_state = {
			# Infrastructure
			effect_destroy_building = { building = building_port }

effect_unmodded_game_setup = {
	effect_historical_obsessions = yes
	every_state = {
		effect_setup_replace_agro_state		= yes
		effect_setup_replace_plant_state	= yes
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_country_type = decentralized } }
		effect_recalc_build_options			= yes
		effect_seed_setup					= yes
	}
	effect_kickstart_trade = yes
	s:STATE_HAWAIIAN_ISLANDS		= { add_state_trait = state_trait_pearl_harbor }
	s:STATE_ERITREA					= { add_state_trait = state_trait_natural_harbors_djibouti }
	s:STATE_ANHALT					= { add_state_trait = state_trait_good_soils }
	s:STATE_BRUNSWICK				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_good_soils }
	s:STATE_RHINELAND				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_saar_coalfield }
	s:STATE_DISTRICT_OF_COLUMBIA	= { if = { limit = { NOT = { is_homeland = cu:yankee } } add_homeland = cu:yankee } }
	s:STATE_PALESTINE				= {
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_homeland = cu:sephardic } } add_homeland = cu:sephardic }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_homeland = cu:ashkenazi } } add_homeland = cu:ashkenazi }
	}
	s:STATE_HEREROLAND				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_namib_desert }
	s:STATE_NAMAQUALAND				= { add_state_trait = state_trait_namib_desert }
	s:STATE_SOUTH_ANGOLA			= { add_state_trait = state_trait_namib_desert }
	s:STATE_ALTO_PARAGUAY			= { remove_state_trait = state_trait_gran_chaco }
	effect_republican_paternalistic = yes
	# every_country = { IPB_non_monarchistizer_effect = yes }	# Removed - Reactionary means Reactionary.
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = empiricism }
		ig:ig_landowners = { if = { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_hierarchic } remove_ideology = ideology_hierarchic add_ideology = ideology_hierarchic_modern } }
	}
	every_country = { effect_reorder_landowner_traits = yes }
	every_state_region = {
		limit = {
			state_is_in_india = yes
			NOT = { is_homeland = cu:anglo_indian }
		}
		add_homeland = cu:anglo_indian
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = c:BIC }
		c:BIC = {
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = india_subject_var } } set_variable = india_subject_var }
			effect_add_anglo_indian_acceptance = yes	# TODO: Potentially makes the game too easy.
			every_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
						NOT = { THIS ?= cu:anglo_indian }
					}
					OR = {
						pop_employment_building_group = bg_government
						is_pop_type = bureaucrats
						is_pop_type = clerks
					}
				}
				change_pop_culture = { target = cu:anglo_indian value = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = c:DEI c:DEI = { NOT = { has_variable = indonesia_subject_var } } }
		c:DEI = { set_variable = indonesia_subject_var }
	}
	effect_decentralize_depopulated_countries = yes
	effect_rebuild_industry = yes
	effect_rebuild_capped = yes
	effect_rebuild_infrastructure = yes
	every_country = {
		switch = {
			trigger = has_technology_researched
			conveyors			= { effect_assembly_lines_switch		= yes }
			rotary_valve_engine	= { effect_rotary_valve_engine_switch	= yes }
			watertube_boiler	= { effect_watertube_boiler_switch		= yes }
		}
		effect_reset_transport_switch	= yes
		effect_transport_switch			= yes
		effect_reset_autarky_pm_state	= yes
		effect_fertilizer_switch		= yes
		effect_tools_switch				= yes
		effect_dynamite_switch			= yes
	}
}
